AI\\u003c/strong\\u003e is a self-hosted, multi-format API gateway proxy that wraps 12 free-tier LLM providers—Gemini, OpenRouter, Cerebras, Groq, Mistral, GitHub Models, SambaNova, Cohere, Cloudflare, Z.ai (Zhipu), HuggingFace, and NVIDIA—into a unified environment.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eThe proxy serves two endpoints natively:\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cul dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003e\\u003cstrong\\u003eOpenAI-Compatible Endpoint (\\u003ccode\\u003e/v1\\u003c/code\\u003e)\\u003c/strong\\u003e: Point OpenAI SDKs or clients at \\u003ccode\\u003e/v1\\u003c/code\\u003e using your unified OpenAI key (\\u003ccode\\u003eomnikey-\\u003c/code\\u003e prefix). Supports \\u003ccode\\u003egenerateContent\\u003c/code\\u003e (chat, vision, audio input), \\u003ccode\\u003estreamGenerateContent\\u003c/code\\u003e, and \\u003ccode\\u003egenerateImages\\u003c/code\\u003e (text-to-image generation).\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003c/ul\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eBehind the scenes, OmniKey AI handles key storage (encrypted using AES-256-GCM), key duplication protection, rate limits, model routing, fallback cascades, and local telemetry logging of daily/monthly token counts.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003chr\\u003e\\n\\u003cdiv class=\\\"markdown-heading\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003ch2 tabindex=\\\"-1\\\" class=\\\"heading-element\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003ca id=\\\"user-content-why-omnikey-ai\\\"\\u003e\\u003c/a\\u003e❓ Why OmniKey AI?\\u003c/h2\\u003e\\u003ca id=\\\"user-content--why-omnikey-ai\\\" class=\\\"anchor\\\" aria-label=\\\"Permalink: ❓ Why OmniKey AI?\\\" href=\\\"#-why-omnikey-ai\\\"\\u003e\\u003csvg data-component=\\\"Octicon\\\" class=\\\"octicon octicon-link\\\" viewBox=\\\"0 0 16 16\\\" version=\\\"1.1\\\" width=\\\"16\\\" height=\\\"16\\\" aria-hidden=\\\"true\\\"\\u003e\\u003cpath d=\\\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\\\"\\u003e\\u003c/path\\u003e\\u003c/svg\\u003e\\u003c/a\\u003e\\u003c/div\\u003e\\n\\u003cblockquote\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003cstrong\\u003eMost developers want access to a variety of models but don't want to pay high costs or manage multiple API keys. (with Topic, entire debate history)\\n        Server--\\u0026gt;\\u0026gt;App: Return Final Verdict \\u0026amp; Declare Winner\\n        App-\\u0026gt;\\u0026gt;App: Append Final Verdict to UI\\n dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eInstead of requiring manual configuration changes inside each calling client whenever a key rate limits or a provider goes down, OmniKey AI acts as an intelligent router:\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003col dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003e\\u003cstrong\\u003eEndpoint Adaptation\\u003c/strong\\u003e: Standard requests targeting the \\u003ccode\\u003e/v1/chat/completions\\u003c/code\\u003e endpoint are parsed, authenticated, and mapped to the target provider's schemas (e.g. OpenAI format). The \\u003ccode\\u003e/v1beta/models/:model\\u003c/code\\u003e endpoint intercepts Gemini-spec payloads and translates them internally to standard messages before processing, then outputs the exact response structure expected by Gemini SDKs.\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003e\\u003cstrong\\u003eProvider Selection\\u003c/strong\\u003e: When a request requests a specific model, OmniKey AI identifies all configured providers that support it. If the virtual model name \\u003ccode\\u003e\\\"auto\\\"\\u003c/code\\u003e is specified, OmniKey AI automatically selects the top-priority provider from your configured Fallback Chain.\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003e\\u003cstrong\\u003eExecution and Failover\\u003c/strong\\u003e: The proxy attempts to execute the API call using the decrypted API credentials. If the upstream provider returns an error (such as a \\u003ccode\\u003e429 Rate Limit\\u003c/code\\u003e or \\u003ccode\\u003e5xx Server Error\\u003c/code\\u003e), the routing engine intercepts the failure, temporarily marks the key/provider as rate-limited, and transparently cascades the request to the next provider in the fallback chain. The downstream application receives a successful response seamlessly.\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003c/ol\\u003e\\n\\u003chr\\u003e\\n\\u003cdiv class=\\\"markdown-heading\\\" id=\"user-content--overview\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 🔍 Overview\" href=\"#-overview\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>OmniKey AI</strong> is a self-hosted, multi-format API gateway proxy that wraps 12 free-tier LLM providers—Gemini, OpenRouter, Cerebras, Groq, Mistral, GitHub Models, SambaNova, Cohere, Cloudflare, Z.ai (Zhipu), HuggingFace, and NVIDIA—into a unified environment.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">The proxy serves two endpoints natively:</p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li><strong>OpenAI-Compatible Endpoint (<code>/v1</code>)</strong>: Point OpenAI SDKs or clients at <code>/v1</code> using your unified OpenAI key (<code>omnikey-</code> prefix). Supports standard chat completions (<code>/v1/chat/completions</code>), vision, transcription (<code>/v1/audio/transcriptions</code>), text-to-speech (<code>/v1/audio/speech</code>), and text-to-image generation (<code>/v1/images/generations</code>).</li>\n<li><strong>Gemini-Compatible Endpoint (<code>/v1beta</code>)</strong>: Point Gemini SDKs or REST clients at <code>/v1beta/models/:model</code> using your unified Gemini key (<code>omnikey-g-</code> prefix). Supports <code>generateContent</code> (chat, vision, audio input), <code>streamGenerateContent</code>, and <code>generateImages</code> (text-to-image generation).</li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Behind the scenes, OmniKey AI handles key storage (encrypted using AES-256-GCM), key duplication protection, rate limits, model routing, fallback cascades, and local telemetry logging of daily/monthly token counts.</p>\n<hr>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"><a id=\"user-content-why-omnikey-ai\"></a>❓ Why OmniKey AI?</h2><a id=\"user-content--why-omnikey-ai\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: ❓ Why OmniKey AI?\" href=\"#-why-omnikey-ai\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<blockquote>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Most developers want access to a variety of models but don't want to pay high costs or manage multiple API keys. OmniKey AI handles this complexity for you.</strong></p>\n</blockquote>\n<markdown-accessiblity-table data-catalyst=\"\"><table>\n<thead>\n<tr>\n<th>Feature</th>\n<th>Manual Multi-Provider Integration</th>\n<th>OmniKey AI</th>\n</tr>\n</thead>\n<tbody>\n<tr>\n<td><strong>API Endpoints</strong></td>\n<td>A dozen different URLs and payload formats</td>\n<td>Dual <code>/v1</code> (OpenAI format) &amp; <code>/v1beta</code> (Gemini format)</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td><strong>API Keys</strong></td>\n<td>12 separate keys to rotate, secure, and manage</td>\n<td>Twin unified keys (<code>omnikey-</code> / <code>omnikey-g-</code>) stored securely</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td><strong>Image Generation</strong></td>\n<td>Manual model hosting or API integration</td>\n<td>Unified <code>/v1/images/generations</code> and <code>/v1beta/models/:model:generateImages</code> routing</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td><strong>Failover</strong></td>\n<td>Manual retry logic; app crashes when provider is down</td>\n<td>Automatic fallback to backup providers in milliseconds</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td><strong>Rate-Limits (429)</strong></td>\n<td>Request fails immediately</td>\n<td>Transparent retry across next best provider</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td><strong>Usage Tracking</strong></td>\n<td>Custom logging per platform to track free caps</td>\n<td>Automatic local tracking of daily/monthly token usage</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td><strong>Privacy</strong></td>\n<td>Credentials stored in plain-text <code>.env</code> files</td>\n<td>AES-256-GCM envelope encryption in local SQLite</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td><strong>Visuals</strong></td>\n<td>CLI-only or no interface</td>\n<td>Modern React-based dashboard with models explorer &amp; class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"><a id=\"user-content-dynamic-routing-and-fallbacks\"></a>🔀 Dynamic Routing and Fallbacks</h2><a id=\"user-content--dynamic-routing-and-fallbacks\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 🔀 Dynamic Routing and Fallbacks\" href=\"#-dynamic-routing-and-fallbacks\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Instead of requiring manual configuration changes inside each calling client whenever a key rate limits or a provider goes down, OmniKey AI acts as an intelligent router:</p>\n<ol dir=\"auto\">\n<li><strong>Endpoint Adaptation</strong>: Standard requests targeting the <code>/v1/chat/completions</code> endpoint are parsed, authenticated, and mapped to the target provider's schemas (e.g. OpenAI format). The <code>/v1beta/models/:model</code> endpoint intercepts Gemini-spec payloads and translates them internally to standard messages before processing, then outputs the exact response structure expected by Gemini SDKs.</li>\n<li><strong>Provider Selection</strong>: When a request requests a specific model, OmniKey AI identifies all configured providers that support it. If the virtual model name <code>\"auto\"</code> is specified, OmniKey AI automatically selects the top-priority provider from your configured Fallback Chain.</li>\n<li><strong>Execution and Failover</strong>: The proxy attempts to execute the API call using the decrypted API credentials. If the upstream provider returns an error (such as a <code>429 Rate Limit</code> or <code>5xx Server Error</code>), the routing engine intercepts the failure, temporarily marks the key/provider as rate-limited, and transparently cascades the request to the next provider in the fallback chain. The downstream application receives a successful response seamlessly.</li>\n</ol>\n<hr>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"><a id=\"user-content-smart-routing-and-limits\"></a>🛡️ Smart Routing and Limits</h2><a id=\"user-content-️-smart-routing-and-limits\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 🛡️ Smart Routing and Limits\" href=\"#️-smart-routing-and-limits\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">To prevent API keys from getting banned or throwing persistent rate limit errors, OmniKey AI applies:</p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li><strong>Token Budget Checks</strong>: Before executing, checking if the current day's token consumption exceeds limits.</li>\n<li><strong>429 Interception</strong>: Catching rate limit headers from upstream responses and dynamically switching providers.</li>\n<li><strong>Cooldown Windows</strong>: Putting a rate-limited key on a temporary cooldown before retrying.</li>\n<li><strong>Project Key Isolation</strong>: Enables enabling/disabling individual project keys and filtering daily analytics per project key, ensuring strict tenant/application usage boundaries.</li>\n</ul>\n<hr>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 featuring columns configuration, name search, sorting, total model counts, and availability check indicators.</li>\n<li><strong>Health Check Status</strong>: Dials showing API latency, key states, and Local</strong>: A shortcut button next to the database status label to instantly toggle between local database mode and cloud mode.</li>\n</ul>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 HMAC-SHA256 hashed password persistence.</li>\n</ul>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"><a Project Keys</strong>: Generate master unified keys or custom project keys (OpenAI-compatible and Gemini-compatible formats). <code>/api/cron-health</code> to receive uptime stats every 2 minutes.</li>\n</ul>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"><a
